formable_nations_mod_category = {

	formable_nations_generic = {

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_global_flag = ai_disabled
			}
		}

		cost = 0
		days_re_enable = 0
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = TAG1
				original_tag = TAG2
				original_tag = TAG3
			}
			always = no
		}

		visible = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = unform_available
				has_global_flag = hide_formable_nations
			}

			always = no
		}
		available = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = { is_subject = no has_global_flag = puppets_allowed }

					count_triggers = {
						amount = 4#90% rounded up, continents and other exceptions don't use this
						1 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
						1 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
						1 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
						1 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
					}
				}
				#AND = {
					#	is_ai = no
					#	has_global_flag = debug_mode
					#}
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = not_already_existing_tt NOT = { any_country = { has_cosmetic_tag = FORM_NEWTAG } } }
		}
		icon = generic_form_nation
		complete_effect = { #capital_scope = { set_state_flag = formable_nations_old_capital }
			set_cosmetic_tag = FORM_NEWTAG
			add_stability = 0.2
			set_capital = { state = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = core_land
			hidden_effect = { sound_effect = complete_focus  set_country_flag = unform_available
				1 = { add_core_of = ROOT }
			}
			1 = { add_core_of = ROOT }
		}
	}
	no_cosmetic_tag = {

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
		}
		cost = 0
		allowed = {
			always = no
		}
		visible = {
			always = no
			is_ai = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = hide_options_formable_nations }
			has_country_flag = unform_available
		}
		days_re_enable = 0

		icon = generic_decision
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = unform_available }
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			add_stability = -0.2
			random_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = formable_nations_old_capital
				}
				clr_state_flag = formable_nations_old_capital
				ROOT = { set_capital = { state = PREV } }
			}
		}
	}
	####################################################################
	formable_nations_africa = {#continent

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_global_flag = ai_disabled
			}
		}

		cost = 0
		days_re_enable = 0

		allowed = {
			is_ai1 = yes
		}
		visible = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = unform_available
				has_global_flag = hide_formable_nations
			}
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = no_limit
				capital_scope = { is_on_continent = africa }
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_on_continent = africa
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = africa_formable_requirement_tt
				AND = {  #her stateti kontrol ediyo her ülke visible ı atlıyor oyun o yüzden erkenden bitirecek bir check burda
					controls_state = 275 #Pretoria
					controls_state = 448 #Tripoli
					controls_state = 298 #Liberia
					controls_state = 907 #Cairo
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						all_state = {
							OR = {
								AND = {
									is_on_continent = africa
									NOT = {
										OR = {
											state = 697
											state = 178
											state = 702
											state = 703
											state = 704
											state = 297
											state = 705
											state = 708
											state = 709
											state = 706
											state = 707
											state = 713
											state = 543
											state = 906
										}
									}
									is_controlled_by = ROOT
								}
								NOT = { is_on_continent = africa }
								state = 697
								state = 178
								state = 702
								state = 703
								state = 704
								state = 297
								state = 705
								state = 708
								state = 709
								state = 706
								state = 707
								state = 713
								state = 543
								state = 906
							}
						}
					}
					#AND = {
						#	is_ai = no
						#	has_global_flag = debug_mode
						#}
				}
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = not_already_existing_tt NOT = { any_country = { has_cosmetic_tag = FORM_AFR } } }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = is_puppet_tt OR = { is_subject = no has_global_flag = puppets_allowed } }
		}
		icon = formable_nations_africa
		complete_effect = { #capital_scope = { set_state_flag = formable_nations_old_capital }
			set_cosmetic_tag = FORM_AFR
			add_stability = 0.2
			custom_effect_tooltip = core_land
			hidden_effect = { sound_effect = complete_focus  set_country_flag = unform_available
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_on_continent = africa
						NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					}
					add_core_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
	}
